Stakes the contribution; it does not write the lit review. - **pubar-rebuttal**: Use when writing the response to a Public Administration Review (PAR) revise-and-resubmit. Structures the response letter; it does not fabricate new results. - **pubar-review-process**: Use when you need to understand how Public Administration Review (PAR) evaluates a manuscript — double-blind review, desk screening for fit/scope/practice-relevance, the reject / major / minor / accept decision spectrum, the role of Evidence for Practice, and the article types. Sets expectations and shapes the paper to survive review; it does not contact editors. ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/Public-Administration-Review-Skills/skills/pubar-review-process) · ★1.1k)
- **pubar-submission**: Use when running the final pre-submission preflight for Public Administration Review (PAR) — article-type selection, double-blind preparation, the ≤ 8,000-word and ≤ 150-word-abstract caps, Evidence for Practice points, APA formatting, and TOP transparency materials. Final checks; it does not draft content. ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/Public-Administration-Review-Skills/skills/pubar-submission) · ★1.1k)
- **pubar-tables-figures**: Use when building tables and figures for a Public Administration Review (PAR) manuscript so exhibits are self-contained, accessible, and communicate effect magnitude to scholars and practitioners alike. ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/Public-Administration-Review-Skills/skills/pubar-tables-figures) · ★1.1k)
- **pubar-theory-building**: Use when building the theoretical argument of a Public Administration Review (PAR) manuscript into a contribution that advances public-management theory and informs practice. - **pubar-topic-selection**: Use when deciding whether a public-administration project fits Public Administration Review (PAR) and which article type to target.
